, satisfying Mon1 (
13
), and
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\},\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=0\leq P(s^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying Mon2 (
14
).

•

Because
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=(s_{t},a_{t})
, CS (
15
) doesn’t apply.

•

0\leq\theta_{u_{t}}\leq 1,\forall u_{t}
, satisfying (
16
).

•

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}P(s^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=1
, satisfying (
17
).

Therefore,

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{LB}(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=1

and
\forall\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}
:

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{LB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=0

∎

D.1.5.
CF Bounds for Disjoint
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

To enhance readability, we split the proof for Theorem
4.2
into two separate theorems: Theorem
D.3
proves the upper counterfactual probability bound, and Theorem
D.4
proves the lower counterfactual probability bound.

Theorem D.3.

For outgoing transitions from state-action pairs
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
which have completely disjoint support from the observed
(s_{t},a_{t})
, the linear program will produce an upper bound of:

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\begin{cases}\dfrac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}&\text{if $P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})<P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})$}\\
1&\text{otherwise}\\
\end{cases}

for all next states
\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}
.

Proof.

Take an arbitrary transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow\widetilde{s}^{\prime}
where
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
has disjoint support with the observed state-action pair
(s_{t},a_{t})
. By definition:

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\max_{\theta}\left(\frac{\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\right)

Because
P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
(the interventional probability of the observed transition) is fixed, to find the upper bound for
\widetilde{P}_{t}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
we need to maximise
\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}
. From Lemma
D.9
, we have:

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}\leq\min\left(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}),P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\right)

Therefore, if we can show that there exists a
\theta
such that

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}=\min\left((P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}),P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\right)

and
\theta
satisfies the constraints of the optimisation problem, we know that this results in the maximum possible counterfactual transition probability
\widetilde{P}_{t}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
. Since
\forall s,s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S},\forall a\in\mathcal{A},\forall u_{t}\in U_{t},\mu_{s,a,u_{t},s^{\prime}}=1\iff f(s,a,u_{t})=s^{\prime}
by definition of
\mu
, this is equivalent to showing that there exists a
\theta
such that:

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\min\left((P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}),P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\right)

As these notations are equivalent, we will use this second notation for brevity and clarity. Consider the following disjoint cases:

•

P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})<P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

•

P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Case 1:
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})<P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Proof.

In this case, assign
\theta
as follows:

\begin{cases}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=0\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\\
\end{cases}

This assignment of
\theta
satisfies the constraints of the linear optimisation problem, as follows:

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\\
=1-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\}}P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
, therefore it is possible to assign
\theta
such that
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
satisfying (
12
).

•

Mon1 (
13
), Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) are all vacuously true for all transitions from
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
due to Lemma
D.8
.

•

0\leq\theta_{u_{t}}\leq 1,\forall u_{t}
, satisfying (
16
).

•

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\\
+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1
, satisfying (
17
).

Therefore,

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\left(\max_{\theta}\frac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\right)=\frac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}

∎

Case 2:
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Proof.

In this case, assign
\theta
as follows:

\begin{cases}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=0\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
\end{cases}

This assignment of
\theta
satisfies the constraints of the linear optimisation problem, as follows:

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\\
=1-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\}}P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
, therefore it is possible to assign
\theta
such that
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
satisfying (
12
).

•

Mon1 (
13
), Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) are all vacuously true for all transitions from
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
due to Lemma
D.8
.

•

0\leq\theta_{u_{t}}\leq 1,\forall u_{t}
, satisfying (
16
).

•

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\\
+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1
, satisfying (
17
).

All the constraints are satisfied, so this is a valid assignment of
\theta
for this state-action pair. Therefore,

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\max_{\theta}\left(\frac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\right)=\frac{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=1

∎

These two cases can be combined as follows:

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\begin{cases}\frac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}&\text{if $P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))<P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})$}\\
1&\text{otherwise}\\
\end{cases}

for all next states
\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}
.
∎

Theorem D.4.

For outgoing transitions from state-action pairs
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
which have completely disjoint support from the observed
(s_{t},a_{t})
, the linear program will produce a lower bound of:

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{LB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\begin{cases}\frac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-(1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}))}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}&\text{if $P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})>1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})$}\\
0&\text{otherwise}\\
\end{cases}

for all possible next states
\widetilde{s}^{\prime}
.

Proof.

Take an arbitrary transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow\widetilde{s}^{\prime}
where
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
is disjoint from the observed state-action pair
(s_{t},a_{t})
. By definition:

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{LB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\min_{\theta}\left(\frac{\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\right)

Because
P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
(the interventional probability of the observed transition) is fixed, to find the lower bound for
\widetilde{P}_{t}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
we need to minimise
\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}
. Because
\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s^{\prime}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}=1
, this is equivalent to maximising the following equation:

\max_{\theta}\left(\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s^{\prime}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}\right)

From the constraints of the optimisation problem, we have:

(20)

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

and

(21)

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s^{\prime}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}{P(s^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

\forall s,s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S},\forall a\in\mathcal{A},\forall u_{t}\in U_{t},\mu_{s,a,u_{t},s^{\prime}}\in\{0,1\}
(from its definition). Therefore,

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s^{\prime}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}
is maximal when the probability assigned to all
u_{t}
where
\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}=1
and
\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}=0
is maximal:

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}(\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}})\cdot\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s^{\prime}}\leq\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot(\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s^{\prime}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}})\leq\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s^{\prime}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Because of Eq. (
20
) and Eq. (
21
), the probability assigned to all
u_{t}
where
\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}=1
and
\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}=0
cannot be greater than
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
or
1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
. So,

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s^{\prime}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}\leq\min\left(P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}),(1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\right)

Therefore, if we can show that there exists a
\theta
such that

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s^{\prime}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}=\min\left(P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}),(1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\right)

and
\theta
satisfies the constraints of the optimisation problem, we know that this results in the minimum possible counterfactual transition probability
\widetilde{P}_{t}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
. Since
\forall s,s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S},\forall a\in\mathcal{A},\forall u_{t}\in U_{t},\mu_{s,a,u_{t},s^{\prime}}=1\iff f(s,a,u_{t})=s^{\prime}
by definition of
\mu
, this is equivalent to showing that there exists a
\theta
such that:

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\min\left(P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}),(1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\right)

As these notations are equivalent, we will use this second notation for brevity and clarity. Consider the following disjoint cases:

•

P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})>1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

•

P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\leq 1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Case 1:
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})>1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Proof.

In this case,

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\leq 1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

and so

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\geq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-(1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}))

If we can show that there exists a
\theta
such that

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-(1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}))

and
\theta
satisfies the constraints in the optimisation problem, we know that this results in the minimum possible counterfactual transition probability for
\widetilde{P}_{t}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
. We can assign
\theta
as follows:

\begin{cases}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-(1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}))\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=0\\
\end{cases}

This assignment of
\theta
satisfies the constraints of the linear optimisation problem, as follows:

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\\
=1-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\}}P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
, therefore it is possible to assign
\theta
such that
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
satisfying (
12
).

•

Mon1 (
13
), Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) are all vacuously true for all transitions from
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
due to Lemma
D.8
.

•

0\leq\theta_{u_{t}}\leq 1,\forall u_{t}
, satisfying (
16
).

•

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\\
+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1
, satisfying (
17
).

All the constraints are satisfied, so this is a valid assignment of
\theta
for this state-action pair. Therefore,

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{LB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\dfrac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=\frac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-(1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}))}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}

∎

Case 2:
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\leq 1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Proof.

In this case,

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\leq P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

and so

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\geq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0

If we can show that there exists a
\theta
such that

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=0

and
\theta
satisfies the constraints in the optimisation problem, we know that this results in the minimum possible counterfactual transition probability for
\widetilde{P}_{t}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
. We can assign
\theta
as follows:

\begin{cases}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=0\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
\end{cases}

This assignment of
\theta
satisfies the constraints of the linear optimisation problem, as follows:

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\\
=1-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\}}P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
, therefore it is possible to assign
\theta
such that
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
satisfying (
12
).

•

Mon1 (
13
), Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) are all vacuously true for all transitions from
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
due to Lemma
D.8
.

•

0\leq\theta_{u_{t}}\leq 1,\forall u_{t}
, satisfying (
16
).

•

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\\
+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1
, satisfying (
17
).

All the constraints are satisfied, so this is a valid assignment of
\theta
for this state-action pair. Therefore,

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{LB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\dfrac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=\frac{0}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=0

∎

These two cases can be combined as follows:

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{LB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\begin{cases}\frac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-(1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}))}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}&\text{if $P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})>1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})$}\\
0&\text{otherwise}\\
\end{cases}

for all next states
\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}
.
∎

D.1.6.
CF Bounds for
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
with Overlapping Support with
(s_{t},a_{t})

To enhance readability, we split the proof for Theorem
4.3
into two separate theorems: Theorem
D.5
proves the upper counterfactual probability bounds, and Theorem
D.6
proves the lower counterfactual probability bounds.

Theorem D.5.

For outgoing transitions from state-action pairs
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
which have overlapping support with the observed
(s_{t},a_{t})
, but
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\neq(s_{t},a_{t})
, the linear program will produce an upper bound of:

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\begin{cases}\dfrac{\min(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}),P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}&\text{if $\widetilde{s}^{\prime}=s_{t+1}$}\\
0&\text{if $P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0$}\\
&\text{and $\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\geq\dfrac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}$}\\
\min(P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}),1-P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))&\text{if $P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0$}\\
\min(1-P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}),\dfrac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})})&\text{otherwise}\\
\end{cases}

for all possible next states
\widetilde{s}^{\prime}
.

Proof.

Take an arbitrary transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow\widetilde{s}^{\prime}
where
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
has overlapping support with the observed state-action pair
(s_{t},a_{t})
(and
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\neq(s_{t},a_{t})
). Consider the following distinct cases:

•

\widetilde{s}^{\prime}=s_{t+1}

•

\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\neq s_{t+1}
,
\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\geq\dfrac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}
and
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0

•

\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\neq s_{t+1}
,
\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}<\dfrac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}
and
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0

•

\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\neq s_{t+1}
and
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=0

These cases are disjoint and cover all possible situations.

Case 1:
\widetilde{s}^{\prime}=s_{t+1}

Proof.

Consider the following disjoint cases:

•

P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0

•

P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})>0
and
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

•

P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})>0
and
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})<P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Case 1(a):
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0

Proof.

If
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0
, then

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}=0
to satisfy (
12
). Therefore, the maximum counterfactual probability
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
is:

\max_{\theta}\left(\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}\right)=0

If we can show that there exists a
\theta
such that

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=0

and
\theta
satisfies the constraints in the optimisation problem, we know that this results in the maximum possible counterfactual transition probability for
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
. We can assign
\theta
as follows:

\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\\

\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot(1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}))\\

which results in a counterfactual probability of
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=0
.

This assignment of
\theta
satisfies the constraints of the linear optimisation problem, as follows:

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\widetilde{P}_{t}(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\dfrac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\\
=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
so Mon1 (
13
) holds.

•

\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}
:

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}|s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

\displaystyle\leq P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

so Mon2 (
14
) holds.

•

CS (
15
) doesn’t apply for the transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow s_{t+1}
. Then,
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}
, either
P(s^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=0
(so CS (
15
) holds vacuously), or
P(s^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0
and either:

–

P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})>0
, meaning
\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}<\dfrac{P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}
and therefore CS (
15
) holds vacuously.

–

or
P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0
, so the counterfactual probability for this transition must be
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0
, which satisfies CS (
15
) since
\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=\dfrac{P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=0
.

so CS (
15
) holds
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}
.

•

0\leq\theta_{u_{t}}\leq 1,\forall u_{t}
, satisfying (
16
).

•

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=1
, satisfying (
17
).

Therefore,

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\max_{\theta}\left(\dfrac{\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\right)=\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}

∎

Case 1(b):
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})>0
and
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Proof.

From Lemma
D.9
, we have:

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\leq\min(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}),P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))

Therefore,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\leq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

If we can show that there exists a
\theta
such that

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

and
\theta
satisfies the constraints in the optimisation problem, we know that this results in the maximum possible counterfactual transition probability for
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
. We can assign
\theta
as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

\displaystyle=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

\displaystyle=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

\displaystyle=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

This assignment of
\theta
satisfies the constraints of the linear optimisation problem, as follows:

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\widetilde{P}_{t}(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\dfrac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=1\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
so Mon1 (
13
) holds.

•

\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}
:

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}|s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{0}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\leq P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

so Mon2 (
14
) holds.

•

CS (
15
) doesn’t apply for the transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow s_{t+1}
.
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\},\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0

which is guaranteed to satisfy CS (
15
).

•

0\leq\theta_{u_{t}}\leq 1,\forall u_{t}
, satisfying (
16
).

•

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=1
, satisfying (
17
).

Therefore,

\max_{\theta}\left(\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}\right)=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

and

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\max_{\theta}\left(\dfrac{\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\right)=\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=1

∎

Case 1(c):
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})>0
and
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})<P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Proof.

From Lemma
D.9
, we have:

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\leq\min(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}),P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))

Therefore,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\leq P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

If we can show that there exists a
\theta
such that

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

and
\theta
satisfies the constraints in the optimisation problem, we know that this results in the maximum possible counterfactual transition probability for
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
. We can assign
\theta
such that:

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

\displaystyle=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

\displaystyle=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

However, it is not immediately clear whether this assignment of
\theta
is possible, because the Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) constraints limit
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}
for all
s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}
. In particular, there exists a set
S_{CS}\subset\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}
of states which must have a counterfactual transition probability
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0
to satisfy CS (
15
) (i.e.,
\forall s^{\prime}\in S_{CS},\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\geq\dfrac{P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}
and
P(s^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0
). This means that
\forall s^{\prime}\in S_{CS}
we must assign
\theta
such that:

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

\displaystyle=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

As this results in
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0
:

(22)

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}|s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{0}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle=0

Let
S_{other}
be the set of all other states
s\in\mathcal{S}\setminus{\{s_{t+1}\}\cup S_{CS}}
.
\forall s^{\prime}\in S_{other}
, if
P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})>0
and
P(s^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0
, then
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\leq{P}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
to satisfy Mon2 (
14
). Because we do not know whether
P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})>0
and
P(s^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0
for all
s^{\prime}\in S_{other}
, we have to assume that this condition holds for all
s^{\prime}\in S_{other}
, and assign
\theta
with this assumption. This means,
\forall s^{\prime}\in S_{other}
:

(23)

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

\displaystyle\leq P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

\displaystyle\geq P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-(P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}))

as this ensures each counterfactual transition probability satisfies Mon2 (
14
), as follows:

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}|s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

Therefore, to show that there exists a valid assignment of
\theta
, we must prove that under the Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) constraints:

\max_{\theta}\left(\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\right)\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

This is proven in Lemma
D.11
. Now, we can prove that this assignment of
\theta
satisfies the constraints of the linear optimisation problem, as follows:

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
, therefore it is possible to assign
\theta
such that

\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\widetilde{P}_{t}(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\dfrac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
so Mon1 (
13
) holds.

•

Lemma
D.11
proves that we can assign
\theta
such that Mon2 (
14
) holds.

•

CS (
15
) doesn’t apply to the transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow s_{t+1}
. Lemma
D.11
proves that we can assign
\theta
such that CS (
15
) is satisfied for all other transitions.

•

0\leq\theta_{u_{t}}\leq 1,\forall u_{t}
, satisfying (
16
).

•

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=1
, satisfying (
17
).

Therefore,

\max_{\theta}\left(\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}\right)=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

and

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\dfrac{\max_{\theta}\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}

∎

These three sub-cases can be simplified to:

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\dfrac{\min\left(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}),P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\right)}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}

∎

Case 2:
\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\neq s_{t+1}
and
\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\geq\dfrac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}
and
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0

Proof.

Because
\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\geq\dfrac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}
and
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0
, to satisfy CS (
15
) we must have
\widetilde{P}_{t}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0
, therefore
\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0
. From Case 1, we know that we can find an assignment of
\theta
that satisfies CS (
15
) for all transitions, when finding the upper bound of the transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow s_{t+1}
. Therefore, we have already proven in Case 1 that there exists an assignment of
\theta
where
\widetilde{P}_{t}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0
, that satisfies the constraints (
12
)-(
17
). Therefore, there exists an assignment of
\theta
that satisfies
\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0
.
∎

Case 3:
\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\neq s_{t+1}
,
\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}<\dfrac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}
, and
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0

Proof.

Because
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0
,
\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\leq P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
to satisfy Mon2 (
14
). Therefore,

(24)

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}\leq P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

so that

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\dfrac{\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\leq\dfrac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

Clearly, this is less than the maximum possible counterfactual probability proven in Lemma
D.9
:

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}\leq P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\leq\min\left(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}),P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\right)

Also,
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
to satisfy Mon1 (
13
). Therefore,

(25)

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Since
\forall s,s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S},\forall a\in\mathcal{A},\forall u_{t}\in U_{t},\mu_{s,a,u_{t},s^{\prime}}=1\iff f(s,a,u_{t})=s^{\prime}
by definition of
\mu
, Eq. (
24
) and Eq. (
25
) are equivalent to:

(26)

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\leq P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

and

(27)

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

respectively. As these notations are equivalent, we will use this second notation for brevity and clarity. Consider the following disjoint cases:

•

P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))<P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

•

P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))\geq P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Case 3(a):
P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))<P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Proof.

We can assign
\theta
as follows:

\begin{cases}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))\\
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=0\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
\end{cases}

This assignment of
\theta
satisfies the constraints of the linear optimisation problem, as follows:

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (

).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
,

satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
,

satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\\
+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=1-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\}}P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
,

therefore it is possible to assign
\theta
such that

f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
,
satisfying (
12
).

(

) holds, therefore Mon1 (

\theta
for this state-action pair.

Case 3(b):
P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))\geq P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Proof.

If
P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))\geq P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, this implies
P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\geq P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
. Therefore, we can assign
\theta
as follows:

\begin{cases}P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\leq P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\\
0\leq\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\leq\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\end{cases}

s.t.
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
.

We do not know exactly how much probability will be assigned to
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}
and
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}
. However, if
\theta

\theta
will always satisfy the constraints of the optimisation problem, as follows:

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

•

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

).

CS (

(from conditions of Case 3).
