To ensure that Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) hold for all other transitions, we must consider two possible cases:

–

If
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, then we can assign
\theta
such that:

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=0

and

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

which must be possible in our given assignment of
\theta
because from the condition of Case 3(b) we have:

\displaystyle P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))\geq P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle\implies P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}))\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

Because
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=0
,
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=0
, and so
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\},\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0
. This satisfies the Mon2 (
14
) (since
0\leq\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
) and CS (
15
) constraints for all other transitions
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow s^{\prime}
.

–

Otherwise, we must have
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})<P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
. We need to prove that there exists an assignment of
\theta
where the Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) constraints are satisfied for all transitions
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow s^{\prime},\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}
, which equates to proving:

\max_{\theta}\left(\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\right)\geq\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

since

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\leq\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

in our given assignment of
\theta
. From Lemma
D.11
, we have:

(28)

\max_{\theta}\left(\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\right)\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

under the Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) constraints. Since we have assigned the maximum possible probability to
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
under the Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) constraints in our given assignment of
\theta
, (
28
) implies:

\max\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Therefore, we know that we can assign
\theta
such that
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, and all transitions satisfy the Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) constraints.

•

0\leq\theta_{u_{t}}\leq 1,\forall u_{t}
, satisfying (
16
).

•

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=1
, satisfying (
17
).

All the constraints are satisfied, so this is a valid assignment of
\theta
for this state-action pair.
∎

These two cases can be simplified to:

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\min(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})),P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t}))}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle=\min(1-P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}),P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))

∎

Case 4:
\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\neq s_{t+1}
and
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=0

Proof.

Because
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=0
, then the monotonicity and counterfactual stability constraints are vacuously satisfied for
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow\widetilde{s}^{\prime}
. Therefore, as proven in Lemma
D.9
:

(29)

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},\widetilde{s}^{\prime}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}\leq\min\left(P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}),P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\right)

Also,
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
because of the monotonicity constraint, therefore:

(30)

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\mu_{\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\mu_{s_{t},a_{t},u_{t},s_{t+1}}\cdot\theta_{u_{t}}\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

Since
\forall s,s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S},\forall a\in\mathcal{A},\forall u_{t}\in U_{t},\mu_{s,a,u_{t},s^{\prime}}=1\iff f(s,a,u_{t})=s^{\prime}
by definition of
\mu
, Eq. (
29
) and Eq. (
30
) are equivalent to:

(31)

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\leq\min\left(P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}),P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\right)

and

(32)

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

respectively. As these notations are equivalent, we will use this second notation for brevity and clarity. Consider the following disjoint cases:

•

P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))<P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

•

P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))\geq P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

Case 4(a):
P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))<P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

Proof.

We can assign
\theta
as follows:

\begin{cases}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))\\
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=0\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
\end{cases}

This assignment of
\theta
satisfies the constraints of the linear optimisation problem, as follows:

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
,

satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
,

satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\\
=1-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\}}P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
, therefore it is possible to assign
\theta
such that
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
, satisfying (
12
).

(
32
) holds, therefore Mon1 (
13
) is satisfied.

•

Because
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=0
, Mon2 (
14
) holds for the transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow\widetilde{s}^{\prime}
. Also, because
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

. Therefore, Mon2 (

) is satisfied.

•

CS (

) doesn’t apply to the transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow s_{t+1}

because

f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\

, the counterfactual probability of all other transitions

where

0\leq\theta_{u_{t}}\leq 1,\forall u_{t}

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\

f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\

\theta
for this state-action pair.
∎

Case 4(b):
P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))\geq P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

Proof.

We can assign
\theta
as follows:

\begin{cases}P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\leq P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
0\leq\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\leq\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=0\\
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\end{cases}

s.t.
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
.

We do not know exactly how much probability will be assigned to
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}
and
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}
. However, if
\theta
satisfies the above assignment then
\theta
will always satisfy the constraints of the optimisation problem, as follows:

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\

f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
,

satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
,

satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\\
=1-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\}}P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
, therefore it is possible to assign
\theta
such that
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

(
32
) holds, therefore Mon1 (
13
) is satisfied.

•

Because
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=0
, Mon2 (
14
) holds for
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow\widetilde{s}^{\prime}
.

•

CS (
15
) doesn’t apply to the transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow s_{t+1}
, and is vacuously satisfied for the transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow\widetilde{s}^{\prime}
because
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=0
.

•

To ensure that Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) hold for all other transitions, we must consider two possible cases:

–

If
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, then we can assign
\theta
such that:

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=0

and

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}

which must be possible in our given assignment of
\theta
because, from the condition of Case 4(b), we have:

\displaystyle P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}))\geq P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

\displaystyle\implies P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

Because
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=0
,
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=0
, and so
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\},\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=0
. This satisfies the Mon2 (
14
) (since
0\leq\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
) and CS (
15
) constraints for all other transitions
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow s^{\prime}
.

–

Otherwise, we must have
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})<P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
. We need to prove that there exists an assignment of
\theta
where the Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) constraints are satisfied for all transitions
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow s^{\prime},\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}
, which equates to proving:

\max_{\theta}\left(\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\right)\geq\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

since

\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\leq\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

in our given assignment of
\theta
. From Lemma
D.11
, we have:

(33)

\max_{\theta}\left(\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\right)\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

under the Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) constraints. Since we have assigned the maximum possible probability to
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
under the Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) constraints in our given assignment of
\theta
, (
33
) implies:

\max_{\theta}\left(\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\right)\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

Therefore, we know that we can assign
\theta
such that
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, and all transitions satisfy the Mon2 (
14
) and CS (
15
) constraints.

•

0\leq\theta_{u_{t}}\leq 1,\forall u_{t}
, satisfying (
16
).

•

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime},s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=1
, satisfying (
17
).

All the constraints are satisfied, so this is a valid assignment of
\theta
for this state-action pair.
∎

Therefore, these two cases can be simplified to:

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\min\left(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-(P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})),P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\right)}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle=\min\left(1-P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}),\dfrac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\right)

∎

We have proven all the cases for Theorem
D.5
, therefore Theorem
D.5
holds.

∎

Theorem D.6.

For outgoing transitions from state-action pairs
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
which have overlapping support with the observed
(s_{t},a_{t})
, but
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\neq(s_{t},a_{t})
, the linear program will produce a lower bound of:

\widetilde{P}_{t}^{LB}(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})=\begin{cases}\max(P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}),1-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})})&\text{if $\widetilde{s}^{\prime}=s_{t+1}$}\\
0&\text{if $P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0$}\\
&\text{and $\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\geq\dfrac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}$}\\
\max(0,1-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\}}{\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})})&\text{otherwise}\\
\end{cases}

Proof.

Take an arbitrary transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow\widetilde{s}^{\prime}
where
(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
has overlapping support with the observed state-action pair
(s_{t},a_{t})
. Consider the following disjoint cases:

•

\widetilde{s}^{\prime}=s_{t+1}

•

\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\neq s_{t+1}
,
\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}\geq\dfrac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}
and
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})>0

•

\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\neq s_{t+1}
, and
\dfrac{P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}<\dfrac{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}{P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})}
or
P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid s_{t},a_{t})=0

These cases are disjoint and cover all possible situations.

Case 1:
\widetilde{s}^{\prime}=s_{t+1}

Proof.

To satisfy Mon1 (
13
),
\widetilde{P}_{t}^{LB}(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
. To do this, we must assign
\theta
such that:

(34)

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})

Consider the following disjoint cases:

(1)

P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq 1-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}

(2)

P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})<1-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}

Case 1(a):
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq 1-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}

Proof.

In this case,
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq 1-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}
, therefore:

(35)

P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\geq P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}

Therefore, we can assign
\theta
as follows:

\begin{cases}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\\
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\end{cases}

This assignment of
\theta
satisfies the constraints of the linear optimisation problem, as follows:

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=1-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (

).

f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\

\theta
such that
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\},\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

, satisfying (

).

) holds, so Mon1 (

) is satisfied.

CS (

) doesn’t apply to the transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow s_{t+1}
.

From (
35
), we get:

P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})-P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(\widetilde{s}^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\leq\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})

This means that
\theta
can be assigned such that
\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}
, the counterfactual probability of each transition
\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a}\rightarrow s^{\prime}
will be
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\leq\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
. We have already proven that each
\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
satisfies the (

) and CS (
15
) constraints, and because
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})\leq\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
,
\widetilde{P}_{t}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})
must satisfy CS as well.

0\leq\theta_{u_{t}}\leq 1,\forall u_{t}
, satisfying (

).

\sum_{u_{t}=1}^{|U_{t}|}\theta_{u_{t}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

\theta
for this state-action pair.

Case 1(b):
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})<1-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}

Proof.

In this case
P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})<1-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}
, therefore:

(36)

P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})<P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot(1-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})})

Therefore, we can assign
\theta
as follows:

\begin{cases}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot(1-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})})\\
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})\cdot(\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{\widetilde{P}_{t}^{UB}(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})})\\
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=P(s_{t+1}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}=\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}{P(s^{\prime}\mid\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a})}-\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
\end{cases}

This assignment of
\theta
satisfies the constraints of the linear optimisation problem, as follows:

•

f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{\theta_{u_{t}}}\\
=P(s_{t+1}\mid s_{t},a_{t})
, satisfying (
12
).

•

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\

f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
f(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{a},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\end{subarray}}\theta_{u_{t}}+\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{s_{t+1}\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{t}\in U_{t}\\
f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\

f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})=s_{t+1}\\

f(s_{t},a_{t},u_{t})\neq s_{t+1}\\
